Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The stereochemistry variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 450 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 481 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the stereochemistry space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 450 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 481. QED.
